A grown-up-sized long-sleeved belted printed one-piece zipper leisure suit in double-knit polyester, with flared bell-bottom legs, covered edge to edge in an all-over sublimation print that covers the entire shirt, designed to look like the outfit of a ghost bride. The print covers every part of the garment edge to edge, from the neckline to the hem of the bottom. The top shows a faded wedding gown with a veil. An attached hood at the neck is printed as a veil. Front and back are both fully printed, the back showing how the outfit looks from behind. The print is cut to the garment's exact shape, so the neck, shoulders, sleeves and leg openings are all covered in the outfit's own fabric. The print is cut exactly to the garment, neck, shoulders, armholes, sleeves and hem are all covered in the outfit's own fabric. The suit is a single piece with a front zipper, a wide collar and a fitted waist seam, the print continuing from top to ankles. The garment is solid opaque cotton and every texture and sheen is a printed picture. The print is the garment itself, flat and unfolded over the garment, in its own cloth and colours. It is a complete costume of ghost bride in one piece, shown full length from collar to ankles, with a faded wedding gown with a veil, a veil (as the hood), a wilted bouquet sewn or attached to the suit. The upper half carries the top and the leg tubes carry the lower garment, so the whole outfit reads at once. Novelty embellishments, satin ribbon bows and piping and fabric flames, stars or stripes sewn along the sleeves and legs. The embellishments are sewn on as real raised fabric, so the suit has depth, texture and sparkle from every angle.
